# Chinese 02 — Band 2-oriented routine communication

Exactly **600 original text cards and 600 ordered mappings**, `zh-02-0001`–`zh-02-0600`; deck `deck:zh-Hans-CN:02`. All are **P: standard spoken Chinese (Putonghua), Simplified production**. Accepted Chinese 00–01 supplies 600 predecessor cards/maps, so delivered Chinese source totals **1,200 records/maps**. Spanish is not assumed learner knowledge. Stage acceptance is a separate ORCHESTRATOR decision.

Fifteen sequential fresh author/editor pairs reviewed all 600 cards. One exhaustive final editorial pass then covered consecutive partitions 0001–0400 and 0401–0600; verified findings were applied together. Every mapping below records completed review, with no unresolved requirement.

The allocation follows [Chinese plan #3 Stage 02](https://github.com/25d-228/meiki-decks/issues/3#stage-02), [execution issue #29](https://github.com/25d-228/meiki-decks/issues/29), [roadmap #1](https://github.com/25d-228/meiki-decks/issues/1), and the unchanged [pinned guide §6.3](https://github.com/25d-228/meiki-decks/blob/e1d8239b7560f26b092cc7bb97c005738b34361f/languages/zh.md#63-routine-communication). Counts and actual hidden learning are reconciled separately.

| Block | Inclusive counters | P cards | Main scope |
| --- | --- | ---: | --- |
| A | 0001–0110 |110| Completion, change and experience; recent-event lexical predicates |
| B | 0111–0205 |95| Ongoing actions and maintained states; compatible posture/action vocabulary |
| C | 0206–0290 |85| Results, basic literal direction/viewpoint and practical task predicates |
| D | 0291–0370 |80| Degree, comparison, physical qualities and expanded measures |
| E | 0371–0445 |75| Clausal modification, clause relationships and practical explanations |
| F | 0446–0520 |75| Introductory affected objects, transfer recipients, pivotal actors and arrangements |
| G | 0521–0600 |80| Home/services/transport/work/study/relationships and clarification/problem solving |
| Total | 0001–0600 |600| Complete assigned §6.3 families |

**Actual hidden families and distinctions**

The following IDs identify focused retrieval. Other occurrences support the sentence and receive no second teaching credit. Lexical cards within A–F introduce actual new words, senses or collocations in the assigned event/frame; their presence does not imply hundreds of distinct aspect or complement rules.

**Completed action, changed situation and experience.** `zh-02-0001` 了; `zh-02-0002` 了; `zh-02-0003` 过; `zh-02-0041` 了; `zh-02-0042` 了; `zh-02-0043` 不; `zh-02-0044` 过; `zh-02-0045` 有没有; `zh-02-0046` 了多久; `zh-02-0081` 了吗; `zh-02-0082` 过没有; `zh-02-0083` 过一次. Postverbal completion includes a future first-action sequence, not an English past ending. Final phase change includes negative adjective/possession and cessation. Neutral experiential guo differs from lexical crossing guò. Experience questions and counts retain their event/object and keep experiential guo distinct from a generic past-tense marker. Additional focused A components cover experiential negation and cessation at 0004–0005, relative timing at 0011–0012, actual repetition and further-repetition prohibition at 0013–0015, and imminence at 0016. Other A cards supply lexical predicates and time expressions; visible aspect on those lexical cards receives no additional grammar credit.

**Progressive versus durative.** `zh-02-0111` 在; `zh-02-0112` 正在; `zh-02-0113` 着; `zh-02-0114` 开着; `zh-02-0115` 穿; `zh-02-0116` 穿着; `zh-02-0117` 拿着; `zh-02-0118` 同时; `zh-02-0119` 还在; `zh-02-0120` 忙着; `zh-02-0201` 没在; `zh-02-0202` 呢; `zh-02-0203` 着; `zh-02-0204` 放着; `zh-02-0205` 持续. Progressive 在 directly precedes an activity, unlike accepted locative 在. Negated ongoing activity and final ongoing 呢 have their own focused records. Maintained posture/open configuration/wearing/holding contrast with activity or getting into a state; location-first existential 着 and a temporary leave-in-place instruction add actual frames. Simultaneity and lexical duration are distinguished from sequence. Posture, carrying, bodily/social and household/device activities 0121–0200 are lexical retrieval, with visible aspect reviewed for compatibility; no blanket claim that all verbs accept all markers.

**Result components.** `zh-02-0206` 完; `zh-02-0207` 好; `zh-02-0208` 懂; `zh-02-0209` 见; `zh-02-0210` 到; `zh-02-0211` 对; `zh-02-0212` 错; `zh-02-0213` 开; `zh-02-0214` 上; `zh-02-0215` 住; `zh-02-0216` 掉; `zh-02-0217` 光; `zh-02-0218` 干净; `zh-02-0219` 清楚; `zh-02-0220` 坏; `zh-02-0221` 破; `zh-02-0222` 满; `zh-02-0223` 空; `zh-02-0224` 醒; `zh-02-0225` 着; `zh-02-0226` 成; `zh-02-0227` 断; `zh-02-0228` 湿; `zh-02-0229` 红; `zh-02-0230` 紧; `zh-02-0231` 够; `zh-02-0232` 住; `zh-02-0233` 上; `zh-02-0234` 下; `zh-02-0276` 熟. Hosts and events remain recoverable: quantitative completion versus readiness, perception versus comprehension, attainment, correctness/error, opening/closure/attachment/removal, physical holding versus information retention, exhaustive consumption, cleanliness/clarity/damage, filled/emptied states, waking/asleep, transformation/severing/wetting/colour/tightness/sufficiency and cooking result. Contextual result 着 zháo differs from durative zhe. Familiar adjectives or characters do not establish prior retrieval of their new result slot.

**Basic literal path and viewpoint.** `zh-02-0235` 来; `zh-02-0236` 去; `zh-02-0237` 进; `zh-02-0238` 出; `zh-02-0239` 上; `zh-02-0240` 下; `zh-02-0241` 进来; `zh-02-0242` 进去; `zh-02-0243` 出来; `zh-02-0244` 出去; `zh-02-0245` 上来; `zh-02-0246` 上去; `zh-02-0247` 下来; `zh-02-0248` 下去; `zh-02-0249` 回来; `zh-02-0250` 回去; `zh-02-0251` 过来; `zh-02-0252` 过去; `zh-02-0253` 起来; `zh-02-0254` 下; `zh-02-0255` 回; `zh-02-0256` 开; `zh-02-0257` 走; `zh-02-0258` 到; `zh-02-0259` 来; `zh-02-0260` 去. Simple toward/away and inward/outward/upward/downward components lead to literal composed path/viewpoint pairs and return, rising/sitting, separation/removal, delivery attainment and intervening-object placement. Speaker/origin/endpoint relations are explicit in focused direction cards. Temporal过去guòqù contrasts with directionalguòqu. The other C task predicates are lexical. No figurative direction, potential system or full advanced affected-object/passive system is credited here.

**Degree and comparison.** `zh-02-0291` 得; `zh-02-0292` 得; `zh-02-0293` 得很; `zh-02-0294` 极了; `zh-02-0295` 得多; `zh-02-0296` 多了; `zh-02-0297` 一点儿; `zh-02-0298` 怎么样; `zh-02-0299` 不如; `zh-02-0300` 跟; `zh-02-0301` 那么; `zh-02-0302` 这么; `zh-02-0303` 比; `zh-02-0304` 多; `zh-02-0305` 少; `zh-02-0306` 越来越; `zh-02-0307` 还. Action-manner and state-intensity/consequence 得, post-adjective degree, large/small comparative differences, performance questions, equality-standard retrieval, negative comparison, deictic degree, action-performance 比, numerical excess/deficit, increasing change and exceeded-standard 还 have actual focused components. Earlier qualitative 比, equality 一样, 少 few and 多 many remain prerequisites; new frames or differentials are identified rather than relabeled as wholly new concepts. Explicit quality/dimension and practical alternatives accompany lexical 0308–0344.

**Expanded measure/classifier choices.** `zh-02-0345` 套; `zh-02-0346` 对; `zh-02-0347` 副; `zh-02-0348` 顶; `zh-02-0349` 艘; `zh-02-0350` 架; `zh-02-0351` 匹; `zh-02-0352` 头; `zh-02-0353` 棵; `zh-02-0354` 朵; `zh-02-0355` 颗; `zh-02-0356` 粒; `zh-02-0357` 根; `zh-02-0358` 枝; `zh-02-0359` 片; `zh-02-0360` 块; `zh-02-0361` 桶; `zh-02-0362` 盆; `zh-02-0363` 壶; `zh-02-0364` 碟; `zh-02-0365` 串; `zh-02-0366` 封; `zh-02-0367` 首; `zh-02-0368` 篇; `zh-02-0369` 场; `zh-02-0370` 趟. Actual counted nouns, quantities and relevant sets/portions remain outside the classifier target. New selection includes sets/pairs/apparatus/headwear/vessels/aircraft/animals/plants/blossoms/particles/slender items/slices/chunks/containers/strings/letters/songs/texts/events/trips. Piece块 differs from currency块, basinful盆 from basin noun, and event场chǎng from earlier supported rain-episode场cháng. No claim that classifiers are universally interchangeable or rigidly exclusive.

**Clausal modification.** `zh-02-0371` 的; `zh-02-0372` 的; `zh-02-0373` 的. Object-gap and subject-gap clauses precede an explicit noun head; a headless clausal nominalization has a recoverable item set. Only the meaningful linking component is hidden, not an entire relative clause. Accepted possessive/adjectival 的 and earlier supported nominal clauses are acknowledged.

**Cause, condition, sequence and contrast.** `zh-02-0374` 所以; `zh-02-0375` 如果; `zh-02-0376` 就; `zh-02-0377` 要是; `zh-02-0378` 只要; `zh-02-0379` 只有; `zh-02-0380` 才; `zh-02-0381` 虽然; `zh-02-0382` 可是; `zh-02-0383` 不过; `zh-02-0384` 而是; `zh-02-0385` 否则; `zh-02-0386` 接着; `zh-02-0387` 之后; `zh-02-0388` 之前; `zh-02-0389` 首先; `zh-02-0390` 最后; `zh-02-0391` 于是; `zh-02-0392` 因此; `zh-02-0393` 而且; `zh-02-0394` 不但; `zh-02-0395` 直到; `zh-02-0396` 就; `zh-02-0397` 既然; `zh-02-0398` 总之; `zh-02-0399` 例如; `zh-02-0400` 比如. Concrete causes/consequences, hypothetical/sufficient/necessary conditions, concessive and corrective contrasts, otherwise, ordering/boundaries/immediacy, established premises, addition and short explanation/summary/example connectors are focused. Paired clauses keep participants and relationship recoverable. New lexical connector forms are distinguished from an already-known relation; lexical reason/process/reference vocabulary 0401–0445 uses these frames without a second grammar credit.

**Introductory affected objects and recipients.** `zh-02-0446` 把; `zh-02-0447` 没把; `zh-02-0448` 别把; `zh-02-0449` 给; `zh-02-0460` 给; `zh-02-0461` 向. 把 introduces a specified affected object before a bounded action/result, distinct from accepted classifier把. Negation/prohibition precede it. Postverbal and preverbal recipient/benefactive 给 differ from known main-verb giving, and 向 directs an act to an addressee. Marked recipient phrases are not mislabeled as unmarked double objects.

**Common double objects and pivotal participants.** `zh-02-0450` 送; `zh-02-0451` 借; `zh-02-0452` 还; `zh-02-0453` 教; `zh-02-0454` 问; `zh-02-0455` 讲; `zh-02-0456` 付; `zh-02-0457` 赔; `zh-02-0458` 奖励; `zh-02-0459` 递; `zh-02-0462` 请; `zh-02-0463` 让; `zh-02-0464` 让; `zh-02-0465` 叫; `zh-02-0466` 劝; `zh-02-0467` 邀请; `zh-02-0468` 安排; `zh-02-0469` 要求; `zh-02-0470` 陪; `zh-02-0471` 带; `zh-02-0472` 送; `zh-02-0473` 催; `zh-02-0474` 拜托; `zh-02-0475` 托; `zh-02-0476` 留; `zh-02-0477` 提供; `zh-02-0478` 交; `zh-02-0479` 分; `zh-02-0480` 分配. Storytelling 讲 at 0455 uses a marked preverbal listener phrase and receives lexical credit. The other actual recipient/item, learner/content, addressee/question and transfer frames are distinguished from earlier 给 and 告诉 records that already contain two objects. Asking/directing/allowing/inviting/assigning/advising/escorting and entrusting retain the person who performs the following action; earlier 麻烦你+action is acknowledged. Desire/request/permission/commitment is not silently equated with execution. Later F 0481–0520 supplies actual transfer/request/coordination/custody/care vocabulary, not another hidden credit for visible roles.

**Practical lexical and repair functions.** `zh-02-0521` 地址; `zh-02-0525` 行李; `zh-02-0529` 换乘; `zh-02-0531` 排队; `zh-02-0532` 晚点; `zh-02-0533` 退票; `zh-02-0534` 改签; `zh-02-0535` 入住; `zh-02-0536` 退房; `zh-02-0537` 房租; `zh-02-0538` 押金; `zh-02-0539` 发票; `zh-02-0544` 退款; `zh-02-0546` 故障; `zh-02-0548` 充电; `zh-02-0552` 停电; `zh-02-0553` 网络; `zh-02-0554` 密码; `zh-02-0558` 取号; `zh-02-0559` 叫号; `zh-02-0560` 空调; `zh-02-0561` 误会; `zh-02-0562` 原谅; `zh-02-0563` 抱歉; `zh-02-0570` 满意; `zh-02-0579` 打扰; `zh-02-0580` 介意; `zh-02-0581` 含义; `zh-02-0582` 指; `zh-02-0583` 具体; `zh-02-0584` 详细; `zh-02-0587` 漏; `zh-02-0588` 缺少; `zh-02-0589` 多余; `zh-02-0590` 改正; `zh-02-0591` 补充; `zh-02-0592` 修改; `zh-02-0593` 核实; `zh-02-0594` 反馈; `zh-02-0595` 投诉; `zh-02-0596` 商量; `zh-02-0597` 讨论; `zh-02-0598` 协商; `zh-02-0599` 换句话说; `zh-02-0600` 也就是说. Transport/service updates, household/device problems, messages/details, relationship reactions, clarification, missing/extra information, revision, feedback, complaints and negotiated arrangements are grounded in self-contained situations. 含义 adds a lexical form beside accepted 意思; reformulation connectors retain both the original statement and accurate restatement. Fictional local arrangements do not assert current law, prices, service policy or universal cultural practice.

**Progression, breadth and limits**

The records compare actual full accepted 00/01 cards and mappings with earlier 02 retrieval. Known characters can acquire new grammatical roles; conversely, a familiar hidden sense does not become new because a name, object or date changes. Repeated targets here are justified by their actual frame/function/sense, not English-gloss variation. Pending duplicate objectives 擦 and 意思 were replaced before authoring those records; the delivered source instead includes cooking-result 熟 and lexical 含义. Lexical alternatives are reported as forms for established concepts when that is what they add.

The material includes ordinary family/home routines and guests, bodily positions/actions and leisure, meals and household tasks, physical movement and delivery, shopping/quantities/quality/service problems, transport/stays, class/work arrangements, messages/devices, personal choices/relationships, short explanations and practical repair. Row situations and prerequisites identify the actual evidence; breadth is not a fixed per-batch topic quota. A merely visible advanced-looking item receives bounded support where needed and does not certify that later system. The full common 把/被, extended directional/potential, complex topic/rhetorical and other later-stage systems remain unassigned.

**Exact source measures**

Counts below are sets of exact NFC source strings: target=`cloze`, lemma=`lemma`, operational pair=(`lemma`,`meaning`) without accent stripping or semantic merging. English-gloss differences alone do not prove distinct linguistic senses. These are operational comparisons, not dictionary sense counts, recognized-vocabulary totals or acquisition claims.

Han counts use characters whose Unicode names identify CJK unified or compatibility ideographs. Direct-target Han is the union in clozes; sentence Han is the union in full sentences; visible-context Han is the union after removing each card’s one exact cloze span; incidental-only Han is sentence Han minus the direct-target union. Direct and visible sets overlap. Punctuation, Pinyin and annotation-only text are excluded; these are distinct characters, not tokens or stroke/handwriting measures.

| Scope | Cards/maps | Exact targets | Exact lemmas | Operational lemma/meaning pairs | Direct Han | Sentence Han | Visible Han | Incidental-only Han |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| Accepted 00–01 | 600 | 578 | 578 | 600 | 523 | 544 | 369 | 21 |
| New 02 | 600 | 569 | 569 | 600 | 604 | 945 | 717 | 341 |
| Cumulative 00–02 | 1200 | 1104 | 1104 | 1200 | 920 | 1026 | 750 | 106 |

The guide’s approximately 600 recognized characters, 1,270 words/phrases and 200 handwritten characters are separate landmarks, not replacement quotas or caps. Source exposure does not establish recognition, handwriting, listening, spontaneous use, proficiency or examination/CEFR equivalence. No final ten-stage/10,000-card reconciliation is performed here.

**Reading and selective primary evidence**

All 600 readings cover the complete actual target span only, with lexical tones, contextual polyphones, actual neutral syllables and reviewed word/phrase boundaries. Grammatical le/guo/zhe are not replaced by character defaults; result着zháo and lexical/path过guò retain their different functions. The selected weak deictic lai/qu readings are contextual; independent来/去 and temporal过去 retain their lexical readings. Connected-speech changes are separately labeled, without silent 一/不/third-tone substitution or a universal rule for every word containing a character. Classifier个 retains lexical gè; optional weakened speech is distinct.

[GB/T16159–2012, original MOE PDF](https://www.moe.gov.cn/ewebeditor/uploadfile/2015/01/13/20150113091717604.pdf), selectively visually checked in retained primary pages 2–5, supplies word/phrase, verb-aspect/final-modal and complement grouping principles. Reused accepted-stage word-specific decisions remain applicable. Actual whole words and phrases determine boundaries; separability, an unsuccessful lookup or an all-syllables-spaced table is not a general proof.

Original Wheatley MIT sections selectively read for function: [Unit 6 §6.3](https://ocw.mit.edu/courses/res-21g-003-learning-chinese-a-foundation-course-in-mandarin-spring-2011/ce12c83210a6eacc170b3034f922ddde_MITRES_21G_003S11_unit06.pdf) experience; [Unit 7 §7.1–7.2](https://ocw.mit.edu/courses/res-21g-003-learning-chinese-a-foundation-course-in-mandarin-spring-2011/9ac5a3ef1007db50644516dee9a1d249_MITRES_21G_003S11_unit07.pdf) results/literal direction/paired clause relations; [Unit 8 §8.3,8.10,8.12.1](https://ocw.mit.edu/courses/res-21g-003-learning-chinese-a-foundation-course-in-mandarin-spring-2011/3fb7ed18b72df30602aba28f4e94abe6_MITRES_21G_003S11_unit08.pdf) degree/comparison, completion priority and basic deictic direction; [Unit 9 §9.1,9.4,9.7,9.11](https://ocw.mit.edu/courses/res-21g-003-learning-chinese-a-foundation-course-in-mandarin-spring-2011/56eae0bfe4eb82d1e827bc1b19dff93d_MITRES_21G_003S11_unit09.pdf) modification, maintained configuration, introductory affected objects and goal/recipient phrases. Only those actual passages support these uses; examples, potential forms, broad cultural assertions and every textbook transcription were not imported.

Selected indexed original [MOE GF0015–2010](https://www.moe.gov.cn/jyb_sjzl/ziliao/A19/201010/W020220124393643101738.pdf) entries support 打球dǎ qiú, 出汗chū hàn, 进来jìnlai/进去jìnqu and lexical alternatives as specifically reviewed. The attempted full PDF open timed out: indexed rows are actual evidence, not a claim of newly reading the complete PDF or importing its wordlist. The curriculum label is not claimed as its current official examination syllabus.

Individual primary publisher decisions include [上周shàngzhōu](https://www.collinsdictionary.com/dictionary/english-chinese/this-next-last-week), [答应dāying](https://www.collinsdictionary.com/dictionary/chinese-english/答应), [咳嗽késou](https://www.collinsdictionary.com/dictionary/chinese-english/咳嗽), [哈欠hāqian](https://www.collinsdictionary.com/dictionary/chinese-english/哈欠), [打喷嚏dǎ pēntì](https://www.collinsdictionary.com/dictionary/english-chinese/sneeze), [收拾shōushi](https://www.collinsdictionary.com/dictionary/chinese-english/收拾), [转身zhuǎnshēn](https://www.collinsdictionary.com/dictionary/english-chinese/turn), [场cháng/chǎng](https://www.collinsdictionary.com/zh/dictionary/chinese-english/场), and contextual [rotational拧nǐng](https://www.collinsdictionary.com/dictionary/english-chinese/screw) versus wringing níng. [Original MIT Character Lesson 7 §7.2.1 printed 157](https://ocw.mit.edu/courses/res-21g-003-learning-chinese-a-foundation-course-in-mandarin-spring-2011/fd0990f4a876b71715713ecf463f50f8_MITRES_21G_003S11_char07.pdf) attests selected 上次shàng cì. Attested full/weak variants are not categorically banned. Unsettled unnecessary whole-compound transcriptions were simplified to supported new components where appropriate, preserving all required target readings and useful visible support.

Further selected original evidence: [Mango Languages, Lina Shen’s 越来越 section](https://www.mangolanguages.com/resources/learn/grammar/mandarin-chinese/how-to-express-the-more-the-more-in-mandarin-chinese) explicitly attests spaced yuè lái yuè and also a joined variant; the chosen construction spelling does not ban the other. Individual Collins publisher entries attest [尺寸 chǐcun](https://www.collinsdictionary.com/zh/dictionary/chinese-english/尺寸), [新鲜 xīnxiān](https://www.collinsdictionary.com/zh/dictionary/chinese-english/新鲜), [结实 jiēshi](https://www.collinsdictionary.com/zh/dictionary/chinese-english/结实), [划算 huásuàn](https://www.collinsdictionary.com/zh/dictionary/chinese-english/划算), [要是 yàoshi](https://www.collinsdictionary.com/dictionary/chinese-english/要是), [除了 chúle](https://www.collinsdictionary.com/dictionary/chinese-english/除了), and literal [充电 chōngdiàn](https://www.collinsdictionary.com/dictionary/chinese-english/充电). Selected [尽快 jǐnkuài](https://www.collinsdictionary.com/dictionary/chinese-english/尽/related) is also MOE GF individual entry 1258; publisher fourth-tone jìnkuài variants are acknowledged. [稍等 shāo děng](https://www.collinsdictionary.com/dictionary/english-chinese/i-ll-be-with-you-in-a-minute) and full-tone [照顾 zhàogù](https://www.collinsdictionary.com/dictionary/chinese-english/照顾) are word-specific selections. Selected [任务 rènwu](https://www.collinsdictionary.com/dictionary/chinese-english/任务) is also attested by MOE GF individual entry 1368; full rènwù variants are acknowledged. [排队 páiduì](https://www.collinsdictionary.com/dictionary/english-chinese/line-up) is a publisher-attested lexical selection. Indexed publisher entries, actual opened passages and unavailable lookups were distinguished; an entry-specific selection is not a universal transcription rule.

Reference checking was targeted, not a claim that every word or full book was independently looked up. Actual primary reads/selected indexed evidence and failed reads were distinguished. Sentences were authored directly in original Chinese; no guide/textbook/test/corpus sentence or Spanish-master translation was imported. Mechanical checks enforce source/mapping integrity; semantic focus, naturalness, reading accuracy and novelty remain editorial judgments. No audio, runtime, exporter, application or later-stage production is included.
